Let's detail local optimizer.
Function `score_fft(p)`:
```
def score_fft(p):
    n=len(p)
    L=2*n-1
    M=1
    while M < L: M <<=1
    H=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    q=np.fft.irfft(H*H, M)[:L]
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=1e-12: return np.inf
    m=q.max() if q.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```
But p may have negative small? enforce p>=0.

Cache FFT size? Could precompute for n. n fixed for a candidate. Use next_power_of_two. For speed, define class? No closures; can pass n and M? Helpers top-level. Could have global cache? We can define a function returning (n,L,M). Simpler compute each call? Cheap. Numpy rfft computes size each.

For gradient update, need `gradient_active(p, q, beta)` returning c:
- q maybe length L. Compute gamma.
```
def make_weights(q, maxq, gamma):
    w=np.exp(gamma*(q - maxq)) # maxq maybe current max
    w[w < 1e-300]=0
    wsum=w.sum()
    if wsum<=0: w=np.zeros(L); w[0]=1? but maybe active mask
    else w/=wsum
    return w
```
Maybe combine with hard threshold to avoid long tails: `w*= (q >= maxq - 0.05*maxq + ...)`? For smooth, no.

Cross-corr using MMA:

def convolve_with_weights(p, w):
    n=len(p); Mx=... maybe pow2(3*n)
    Hw=np.fft.rfft(w, Mx)
    Hp=np.fft.rfft(p, Mx)

    return c
```

Let's define `local_search` more concretely.

Inputs: p (sum1), q, score, deadline, maybe max_iters.
Keep `current`, `current_score`, `q_current`.
Recompute weights and h:
```
L=2n-1; Mq=nextpow2(L); Mx=nextpow2(3n)
maxq=q_current.max()
gamma=adaptive
w=exp(gamma*(q-current_max)); normalize
h = crosscorr(w,p,Mx)
h = h - h.mean() # for relative
```
Potential h can have large span. For exchange selection, use h raw; sign after mean.
Donors: indices with highest h and p > 1e-6. Recipients: lowest h (could p any). If p_j high maybe okay. To avoid moving to high q peaks? Adaptive.
We also need consider h could be all similar; use random.
Select candidates:
- `top_d = np.argsort(p * np.maximum(h, 0)?)`? Donor should have high h and maybe mass. Use score `h` for p>0. Exclude p=0 for donor? If p=0 nothing. Recipients can be zero; moving to zero might be good (creating sparse). But if all p positive, okay.
- Random pairs: choose random i with p>0, j random (could high h? no). Also gradient pairs: top decile vs bottom decile, sample maybe 20.
For each candidate pair, choose θ. We can attempt exact best θ by optimizing? The objective max of q + a ε + b ε², with ε∈[0,p_i]. Each coefficient q_k is quadratic in ε. We want minimize max. Could solve via binary search? Too heavy. Just try θ fractions [0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0] relative to p_i? If p_i small, 1.0 zero. If p_i large, 1.0 huge. Also try θ to reduce h difference? Since linear term dominates; perhaps optimal θ limited by constraints. Try [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]. For binary, 1.0.
Compute newq and score. Keep best if new_score < current_score - 1e-12.
If accepted, update p: p[j]+=ε; p[i]-=ε; q=newq (clip small negative to 0? q should nonneg but numeric); current_score. Then continue maybe without recomputing h for a few accepts.
If no accept after evaluating E candidates, do a "kick":
- Apply a small gradient multiplicative step (maybe not accepted if worsens exact) or random perturb p by moving random mass: choose random donor/recipient with θ random, accept only if improves? That's already random exchanges.
- Or sharpen: p ^=1.5 normalize? Evaluate; if improves accept.
- Or restart within local from best with a random perturbation.

Exact q update: We need initial q from FFT. For accepted exchange, update q. For large number of accepted moves, q may accumulate small negatives due to floating. Set q[q<0 & q> -1e-12]=0? But score max unaffected if negative. For safety, after e.g. 20 accepts, recompute q from FFT to avoid drift.

We should not use evaluate_sequence inside local (except periodically for global best). Fast score uses sum fixed.

How to compute h efficiently with current p and weights. Use `np.fft.rfft` length Mx. Crosscorr `Hw * Hp.conj()`. But Hp depends on p; if p changes only local, can't update easily; recompute when needed. Mx= nextpow2(3*n) ~32768; H length 16385; multiply; irfft. Cost per iteration maybe 0.01s. Let's set local search loop with time and iteration counts:
```
def local_search(p, q, score, deadline):
    n=len(p); L=2n-1; Mq, Mx = fft_sizes(n)
    # normalize p, recompute q perhaps
    it=0; no_accept=0
    last_h=0
    h=None; w=None
    while time < deadline-2:
       if h is None or it-last_h > 10:
           weights, h = compute_h(p,q,Mx)
           last_h=it; it=0; no_accept=0? 
       # generate up to 30 candidate pairs
       best_pair=None; best_score=score
       for _ in range(num_trials):
           propose (i,j,eps)
           newq, new_score = exchange_score(q,p,i,j,eps)
           if new_score < best_score: ...
       if best_pair accepted:
           apply; no_accept=0; maybe if accepted many skip h recompute
       else:
           no_accept += 1
           if no_accept > stuck_threshold:
               # kick: water-fill g step or random perturb
               p = kick(p,q, score)
           else:
               # perturb weights/gamma? recompute h next
```

```
def make_weights(q, n, gamma=None):
   maxq = q.max()
   if maxq <=0: uniform weights?
   if gamma is None:
      meanq = q.mean()
      spread = maxq-meanq
      if spread<=0: gamma=50*n? else gamma = max(10, min(500000, 20*n/spread))
   w=np.exp(gamma*(q-maxq))
   # Optionally threshold: w *= (q >= maxq - 0.1*maxq) maybe? If gamma high, tails small.
   w[w<1e-300]=0
   sw=w.sum()
   if sw<=0: w=np.ones_like(q); w/=len
   else: w/=sw
   return w
```
Use gamma adaptive maybe based on active count. If spread tiny, gamma big; focus max.
Maybe use two weights: smooth plus exact active? We can use gamma high enough.

Crosscorr h:
```
def corr_weights_p(p,w,Mx):
  n=len(p)
  Hw=np.fft.rfft(w,Mx)
  Hp=np.fft.rfft(p,Mx)
  c=np.fft.irfft(Hw*np.conj(Hp), Mx)[:n]
  return c
```
This c may have small imaginary? irfft real. Normalize? Use as is. For exchange selection, subtract mean? The direction difference uses raw h; subtracting constant doesn't affect d. Use `h = c - c.mean()`.

def propose_pairs(h, p, rng, n, num):
   pos = p > 1e-12
   hpos = h.copy(); hpos[~pos] = -np.inf
   # top donors: np.argpartition(hpos, -num_top)[-num_top:]
   n_top=max(10,n//50)
   top_donors = set(np.argpartition(hpos, -n_top)[-n_top:])
   low_recips = np.argpartition(h, n_low)[:n_low]
   donors=set top + random pos
   recips=set low + random n
   for d in list donors sample maybe 5:
      for r in list recips sample 5:
         if d!=r: yield
```

Let's design local_search loop:
```
def local_search(p, q, score, deadline, rng, global_best):
   n=len(p); L=2n-1; Mx=...
   sp=1.0
   accept_count=0; no_accept=0; last_h=-1000
   best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score; best_q=q.copy()
   # Ensure exact q? recompute maybe
   while time < deadline - 1:
      if no_accept > h_recompute_threshold:
         W,h = compute
         no_accept=0
      # try exchange block
      improved=False
      for _ in range(trials_per_block):

Functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<=1
    return p

def fft_sizes(n):
    L=2*n-1
    M=1
    while M < L: M <<=1
    Mx=1
    while Mx < 3*n: Mx <<=1
    return M,Mx

def conv_square(p):
    n=p.size
    M=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    H=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    q=np.fft.irfft(H*H, M)
    return q[:2*n-1].copy()
```
Copy maybe not needed; q view length. Use `.astype(float, copy=False)`.

`score_array(arr)`:
```
def score_array(arr, conv=None):
    p=np.asarray(arr,float)
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return np.inf
    p=p/s
    if conv is None:
       q=conv_square(p)
    else:
       q=conv
    m=q.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m<0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*len(p)*m/(s*s))? If p normalized then (1)
```
If arr already normalized and q passed, use `2n*m` (with p sum maybe not exact). For consistency use s? But p normalized in place? `p` copy maybe. Better `fast_score(p, q)` assumes p sum1.

```
def fast_score(p, q):
    sp=p.sum()
    m=q.max()
    if sp<=0 or not finite: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*len(p)*m/(sp*sp))
```
If q from p (sum maybe), same.

`normalize(p)`: no copy? We can return new.
```
def normalize_sequence(arr):
    p=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.size==0: return None
    p[~np.isfinite(p)]=0.0
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-15: return None
    p /= s
    s2=p.sum()
    if s2<=1e-15: return None
    p /= s2
    return p
```
Since sum1, max <=1 after normalization; no cap needed.

Kicks implementation:
```
def kick_current(p, q, score, h, rng, gbest, deadline):
   # returns p, q, score, improved
   candidates=[]
   # gradient blends
   if h is not None:
      hshift = h - h.min()
      hshift = hshift / max(hshift.max(),1e-12)
      for beta in (1.0,3.0,10.0):
         g=np.exp(-beta*hshift); g/=g.sum()
         for alpha in (0.2,0.5,1.0): candidates.append((1-alpha)*p + alpha*g)
      for beta in (3.0,10.0):
         g=1.0/(1.0+beta*hshift); g/=g.sum(); for alpha ...
   # sharpen
   for power in (0.5,1.5,2.0,4.0): candidates.append(sharpen(p,power))
   # random noise
   noise=rng.normal(size=p.size); for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2): candidates.append(normalize(p*np.exp(amp*noise)))
   # blend with global best if gbest[1] is not None:
   for alpha in (0.3,0.6): candidates.append((1-alpha)*p + alpha*gbest[1])
   # evaluate each; choose best if score < score
```
This can be many FFTs (3*3+2*3+3+3+2 ~23) ~0.2s. Fine when stuck. If no improvement, maybe after several kicks random restart from global best? Or perturb current strongly: `p = normalize(p + 0.1*global_best?)` and continue.
To avoid endless stuck, track `kick_fails`; if >10, return best and let outer choose next candidate.

   if p is None: return None
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()

   if not finite: return None
   s=p.sum()
   if s<0.01: # evaluator inf if sum<0.01 before clamping? It checks sum a after clipping and cap; sequence sum must >=0.01 else inf.
       # scale to 0.01? Objective scale invariant but evaluator inf if sum<0.01. Need ensure final sum >=0.01.
       # If p sum1 normalized, fine. If fallback, sum 1.
   safest_return = [float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in p]
   return safest_return
```

Let's write `make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(q, n):
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    meanq = float(np.mean(q))
    if maxq <= 0 or not np.isfinite(maxq):
        return np.ones(len(q))/len(q), 0.0
    spread = maxq - meanq
    if spread <= 1e-300: gamma = 10.0 * n
    else: gamma = 3.0 / spread
    gamma = float(np.clip(gamma, 10.0, 30.0 * n))
    w = np.exp(gamma * (q - maxq))
    minw = np.exp(gamma * (meanq - maxq))
    # threshold very small
    small = minw * 1e-6 if minw > 0 else 0.0
    w[w < small] = 0.0
    sw = w.sum()
    if sw <= 0:
       w = np.ones_like(q); sw = len(q)
    else: w = w / sw
    return w, gamma
```
Spreading: meanq for uniform=1/(2n), qmax=1/n, spread=1/(2n)=7e-5, gamma=42k, good. If score better qmax~0.9e-4? spread? okay.

`corr_weights_p`: If Mx next_pow2(3n) computed each time, but in local we pass. Use top-level `corr_weights_p(p,w,Mx)`.

`evaluate_move` function:
```
def evaluate_move(q, p, i, j, eps, L):
    newq = q.copy()
    e2 = eps*eps
    newq[j:j+p.size] += 2.0*eps*p
    newq[i:i+p.size] -= 2.0*eps*p
    ii = 2*i
    if 0 <= ii < L: newq[ii] += e2
    jj = 2*j
    if 0 <= jj < L: newq[jj] += e2
    ij = i+j
    if 0 <= ij < L: newq[ij] -= 2.0*e2
    # because p sum 1, score = 2*n*max(newq)
    m = newq.max()
    if m < 0: m = 0
    return newq, float(2.0*p.size*m)
```
But if p sum drifted, score might differ. We'll maintain p sum exactly. In local search, after accept renormalize p and q if drift.
Use `n=p.size`. The linear slice `newq[j:j+n] += 2.0*eps*p` works even if j+n > L? For L=2n-1, j max n-1, j+n=2n-1=L, okay. `i` similarly.
If i/j indices int. Good.

Potential double counting at overlaps of slices: The linear terms include cross p_i and p_j exactly; if `i` slice and `j` slice overlap, they correctly add. The quadratic terms at `i+j`, `2i`, `2j` handle interactions of e_i and e_j. But if `2i` lies in slice, the linear update already included? The linear update is derived from 2eps shift; quadratic added separately. No conflict.

Let's test move i->j full with p_i>0 p_j maybe. Suppose only p_i=1 (n=1?), old q_i=1 at 0. Move to j=1? formula should yield q at 2j=1? For n=2 with p=[1,0], move full from0 to1: p=[0,1]. q old [1,0,0], new q [0,0,1]. i=0,j=1, eps=1. newq=q. linear slices: add at j=1: newq[1:3]+= [1,1]? p=[1,0], 2eps*p=[2,0], so newq[1]+=2, newq[2]+=0. subtract at i=0: newq[0:2]-=[2,0]: newq[0]-=2, newq[1]-=0. newq before [1,0,0] -> [-1,2,0]. quad: 2i=0 +1 ->0; 2j=2 +1 ->1; i+j=1 -2 ->0. Implement basis in function:
```
def make_random_profile(n, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    scales = []
    # random amplitudes:
    v += rng.normal(0,0.6) * (t-0.5)
    v += rng.normal(0,0.6) * ((t-0.5)**2)
    v += rng.normal(0,0.8) * np.cos(np.pi*t)
    v += rng.normal(0,0.8) * np.sin(np.pi*t)
    for m in (1,2,3,5):
       amp = rng.uniform(0,1.5)/np.sqrt(m)
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       v += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + phase)
    # maybe sharpen by exponentiating: p=exp(v)
    v = v - np.max(v)
    p = np.exp(v)
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    return p
```
This may not produce enough contrast; amp up to1.5, v range maybe few; after exp can be 1-100. Good. Use rng normal. To include zero-density, we can take `p**power` later.

```
def sharpen_profile(p, power):
   if p is None: return None
   z = p ** power
   s = z.sum()
   if s <=0: return None
   z /= s
   return z
```

```
def generate_qr_candidates(candidates, target_n, rng):
   p = find_prime_near(target_n, 4)
   if p is None or p < 7: return
   i=np.arange(p)
   qr = np.zeros(p, dtype=bool)
   qr[(i*i)%p] = True
   qr0 = qr.copy(); qr0[0] = False
   # chirp bool
   chirp = (np.sin(2*np.pi* ((i*i)%p)/p) > 0)
   sets = [qr, qr0, ~qr, ~qr0, chirp, ~chirp]
   for mask in sets:
      # add natural
      add_candidate(candidates, mask.astype(np.float64))
      # affine random 12 each
      for _ in range(12):
          a = int(rng.integers(1, p)); b = int(rng.integers(0, p))
          idx = (a*i + b) % p
          vals = np.zeros(p, dtype=np.float64)
          vals[idx[mask]] = 1.0
          add_candidate(candidates, vals)
```
This is 6*(1+12)=78 candidates; plus transforms maybe. FFT 78 ok. But for `~qr` includes nonzero all true except QR; density half. `~qr0` includes 0 density (p+1)/2. Fine.
Maybe too many if p found large; okay. Could limit 8 random affines. Let's set 10 to save. 6*11=66. Fine.

def is_prime(x):
   if x <2: return False
   small primes? Use trial:
   if x%2==0 return x==2
   if x%3==0 return x==3
   d=5
   while d*d <= x:
      if x%d==0 or x%(d+2)==0: return False
      d +=6
   return True

   for span...
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for low-convolution nonnegative sequences.

Strategy:
- Normalize all candidates to unit sum (objective is scale invariant).
- Generate many diverse starting points: parent, Fourier/block profiles,
  quadratic-residue/chirp sets with affine reindexing, and uniform fallback.
- Run a guided local search:
  * FFT convolution and max-coefficient score.
  * Softmax active-set gradient.
  * Exact O(n) one-pointer mass-transfer moves (exchange  i -> j).
  * Periodic gradient/water-filling-like lambdas and sharpening kicks.
- Keep the best exact-evaluation candidate and validate with evaluate_sequence.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_sizes(n):
    l = 2 * int(n) - 1
    mq = next_pow2(l)
    mx = next_pow2(3 * int(n))
    return mq, mx


def conv_square(p):
    """Full linear convolution p * p, returned as length 2n-1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    mq = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    h = np.fft.rfft(p, mq)
    q = np.fft.irfft(h * h, mq)
    return q[:2 * n - 1].copy()


def fast_score(p, q):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-15 or q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * p.size * m / (s * s))


def score_of(item):
    return item[0]


def normalize_sequence(arr):
    """Return a nonnegative finite numpy array with sum 1, or None."""
    if arr is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    p /= s
    s2 = float(np.sum(p))
    if s2 <= 1e-15:
        return None
    p /= s2
    return p


def add_candidate(candidates, seq):
    p = normalize_sequence(seq)
    if p is None:
        return None
    if p.size > 60000:
        return None
    q = conv_square(p)
    sc = fast_score(p, q)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None
    candidates.append((float(sc), p, q))
    return float(sc)


def sharpen_profile(p, power):
    if p is None:
        return None
    z = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64) ** float(power)
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(z))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    z /= s
    return z


def make_random_profile(n, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    v = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)

    v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.7) * (t - 0.5)
    v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.7) * ((t - 0.5) ** 2)
    v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.9) * np.cos(np.pi * t)
    v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.9) * np.sin(np.pi * t)

    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.4) / np.sqrt(float(m))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)
        v += amp * 0.75 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)

    v -= float(np.mean(v))
    v *= rng.uniform(0.6, 3.0)
    v -= float(np.max(v))
    p = np.exp(v)
    return normalize_sequence(p)


def make_block_profile(n, rng, d):
    d = max(2, int(d))
    levels = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0, d)
    if float(np.sum(levels)) <= 0.0:
        levels[0] = 1.0
    idx = (np.arange(n) * d) / d
    idx = np.minimum(n - 1, np.floor(idx).astype(np.int64))
    p = levels[idx]
    return normalize_sequence(p)


def is_prime(x):
    if x < 2:
        return False
    if x % 2 == 0:
        return x == 2
    if x % 3 == 0:
        return x == 3
    d = 5
    while d * d <= x:
        if x % d == 0 or x % (d + 2) == 0:
            return False
        d += 6
    return True


def find_prime_near(n, preferred_mod):
    max_span = max(2000, int(n) // 3)
    n = int(n)

    if preferred_mod is not None:
        for span in range(0, max_span + 1):
            for x in (n + span, n - span):
                if x < 13:
                    continue
                if x % 4 != int(preferred_mod):
                    continue
                if is_prime(x):
                    return int(x)

    for span in range(0, max_span + 1):
        for x in (n + span, n - span):
            if x < 13:
                continue
            if is_prime(x):
                return int(x)
    return None


def generate_qr_candidates(candidates, target_n, rng):
    p = find_prime_near(target_n, 3)
    if p is None or p < 13:
        p = find_prime_near(target_n, None)
    if p is None or p < 13 or p > 25000:
        return

    i = np.arange(p, dtype=np.int64)
    qr = np.zeros(p, dtype=bool)
    qr[(i * i) % p] = True

    qr_n0 = qr.copy()
    qr_n0[0] = False

    chirp = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * ((i * i) % p) / float(p)) > 0

    masks = [
        qr,
        qr_n0,
        ~qr,
        ~qr_n0,
        chirp,
        ~chirp,
    ]

    for mask in masks:
        vals = np.zeros(p, dtype=np.float64)
        vals[mask] = 1.0
        add_candidate(candidates, vals)

        for _ in range(8):
            a = int(rng.integers(1, p))
            b = int(rng.integers(0, p))
            idx = (a * i + b) % p
            vals = np.zeros(p, dtype=np.float64)
            vals[idx[mask]] = 1.0
            add_candidate(candidates, vals)


def make_weights(q, n):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    if q.size == 0:
        w = np.ones(1)
        return w
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    meanq = float(np.mean(q))
    if maxq <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxq):
        w = np.ones(q.size)
        return w / w.size

    spread = maxq - meanq
    if spread <= 1e-300:
        gamma = 10.0 * n
    else:
        gamma = 3.0 / spread
    gamma = float(np.clip(gamma, 10.0, 30.0 * n))

    w = np.exp(gamma * (q - maxq))
    threshold = np.exp(gamma * (meanq - maxq)) * 1e-8 if meanq > maxq else 0.0
    w[w < threshold] = 0.0
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(q.size)
        sw = float(q.size)
    else:
        w /= sw
    return w


def corr_weights_p(p, w, mx):
    """c_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, i=0..n-1."""
    n = p.size
    hw = np.fft.rfft(w, mx)
    hp = np.fft.rfft(p, mx)
    c = np.fft.irfft(hw * np.conj(hp), mx)
    return c[:n].copy()


def evaluate_move(q, p, i, j, eps, l):
    """Exact new convolution when moving eps mass from index i to index j."""
    n = p.size
    newq = q.copy()
    e2 = eps * eps

    newq[j:j + n] += 2.0 * eps * p
    newq[i:i + n] -= 2.0 * eps * p

    ii = 2 * i
    if 0 <= ii < l:
        newq[ii] += e2

    jj = 2 * j
    if 0 <= jj < l:
        newq[jj] += e2

    ij = i + j
    if 0 <= ij < l:
        newq[ij] -= 2.0 * e2

    m = float(np.max(newq))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return newq, float(2.0 * n * m)


def generate_kick_candidates(p, h, rng, gbest):
    res = []
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)

    if h is not None and h.size == p.size:
        hf = h[np.isfinite(h)]
        if hf.size > 0:
            hmin = float(np.min(hf))
            hmax = float(np.max(hf))
            if hmax > hmin:
                hn = (h - hmin) / (hmax - hmin)
                hn = np.where(np.isfinite(hn), hn, 0.0)
                hn = np.clip(hn, 0.0, 1.0)

                for beta in (1.0, 3.0, 10.0):
                    g = np.exp(-beta * hn)
                    s = float(np.sum(g))
                    if s > 0.0:
                        g /= s
                        for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
                            res.append((1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * g)

                for beta in (3.0, 10.0, 30.0):
                    g = 1.0 / (1.0 + beta * hn)
                    s = float(np.sum(g))
                    if s > 0.0:
                        g /= s
                        for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
                            res.append((1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * g)

    for power in (0.5, 0.75, 1.5, 2.0, 4.0):
        sp = sharpen_profile(p, power)
        if sp is not None:
            res.append(sp)

    z = rng.normal(size=p.size)
    for amp in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40):
        res.append(p * np.exp(amp * z))

    if gbest is not None and len(gbest) > 1 and gbest[1] is not None:
        gb = np.asarray(gbest[1], dtype=np.float64)
        if gb.size == p.size:
            for alpha in (0.10, 0.30, 0.60, 0.90):
                res.append((1.0 - alpha) * p + alpha * gb)
            res.append(gb.copy())

    return res


def local_search(p_in, q_in, score_in, deadline, rng, gbest):
    p = normalize_sequence(p_in)
    if p is None:
        return None, None, np.inf

    q = conv_square(p)
    score = fast_score(p, q)
    n = p.size
    l = 2 * n - 1
    mx = next_pow2(3 * n)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_score = score

    if np.isfinite(score) and score < gbest[0]:
        gbest[0] = float(score)
        gbest[1] = best_p.copy()
        gbest[2] = best_q.copy()

    h = None
    moves_since_h = 0
    no_accept = 0
    accept_count = 0
    kick_fails = 0

    while time.monotonic() < max(0.0, deadline - 2.0):
        if h is None or moves_since_h > 10:
            w = make_weights(q, n)
            h = corr_weights_p(p, w, mx)
            h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
            h -= float(np.mean(h))
            moves_since_h = 0

        pos = p > 1e-12
        ntop = min(max(12, n // 40), n - 1)

        hpos = h.copy()
        hpos[~pos] = -np.inf
        if ntop < n:
            top_d = np.argpartition(hpos, n - ntop)[n - ntop:]
        else:
            top_d = np.where(pos)[0]

        if ntop < n:
            low_r = np.argpartition(h, ntop)[:ntop]
        else:
            low_r = np.arange(n)

        nrand = max(20, n // 80)
        rand_d = rng.integers(0, n, size=nrand)
        rand_r = rng.integers(0, n, size=nrand)

        dset = set(int(x) for x in top_d)
        for x in rand_d:
            if pos[x]:
                dset.add(int(x))
        rset = set(int(x) for x in low_r)
        for x in rand_r:
            rset.add(int(x))

        if not dset:
            dset = {int(rng.integers(0, n))}
        if not rset:
            rset = {int(rng.integers(0, n))}

        max_d = 12
        max_r = 12
        dlist = list(dset)
        if len(dlist) > max_d:
            darr = np.array(dlist, dtype=np.int64)
            dlist = rng.choice(darr, max_d, replace=False).tolist()

        rlist = list(rset)
        if len(rlist) > max_r:
            rarr = np.array(rlist, dtype=np.int64)
            rlist = rng.choice(rarr, max_r, replace=False).tolist()

        best_move = None
        best_move_score = score
        attempts = 0
        trial_limit = 80

        for d in dlist:
            pi = float(p[d])
            if pi <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for r in rlist:
                if attempts >= trial_limit:
                    break
                if int(r) == int(d):
                    continue
                for frac in (0.5, 1.0):
                    eps = pi * frac
                    if eps <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    newq, mv = evaluate_move(q, p, int(d), int(r), eps, l)
                    attempts += 1
                    if mv < best_move_score - 1e-14:
                        best_move_score = mv
                        best_move = (int(d), int(r), eps, newq)
                if attempts >= trial_limit:
                    break
            if attempts >= trial_limit:
                break

        if best_move is not None:
            d, r, eps, newq = best_move
            p[d] -= eps
            p[r] += eps
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)

            q = newq
            m = float(np.max(q))
            if m < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
                m = 0.0
            score = float(2.0 * n * m)

            accept_count += 1
            moves_since_h += 1
            no_accept = 0

            if accept_count % 10 == 0:
                p = normalize_sequence(p)
                q = conv_square(p)
                score = fast_score(p, q)

            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
                best_score = float(score)
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_q = q.copy()
                if best_score < gbest[0]:
                    gbest[0] = best_score
                    gbest[1] = best_p.copy()
                    gbest[2] = best_q.copy()
            continue

        no_accept += 1
        moves_since_h += 1

        if no_accept >= 4 or kick_fails >= 3:
            no_accept = 0
            kick_list = generate_kick_candidates(p, h, rng, gbest)
            if len(kick_list) > 24:
                kick_list = kick_list[:24]

            improved = False
            for kp in kick_list:
                kp = normalize_sequence(kp)
                if kp is None or kp.size != n:
                    continue
                kq = conv_square(kp)
                ksc = fast_score(kp, kq)
                if np.isfinite(ksc) and ksc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    p = kp
                    q = kq
                    score = float(ksc)
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_q = q.copy()
                    best_score = score
                    if best_score < gbest[0]:
                        gbest[0] = best_score
                        gbest[1] = best_p.copy()
                        gbest[2] = best_q.copy()
                    improved = True
                    h = None
                    moves_since_h = 100
                    break

            if improved:
                kick_fails = 0
                continue

            kick_fails += 1
            if kick_fails >= 10:
                break

    return best_p, best_q, float(best_score)


def sequence_from_array(arr):
    if arr is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(p)) < 0.01:
        # scale up if necessary; objective is scale invariant for positive sum
        if p.size > 0:
            p *= 0.02
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def authoritative_score(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        p = normalize_sequence(seq)
        if p is None:
            return np.inf
        q = conv_square(p)
        return fast_score(p, q)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Generate a strong candidate sequence under the given time budget."""
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, budget_s - 18.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    parent_seq = None
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            parent_seq = [float(x) for x in parent]
        except Exception:
            parent_seq = None

    if parent_seq is not None and 512 <= len(parent_seq) <= 25000:
        n_main = int(len(parent_seq))
    else:
        n_main = 7000

    candidates = []

    if parent_seq is not None:
        add_candidate(candidates, parent_seq)

    add_candidate(candidates, np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64))

    # Random smooth profiles and block profiles.
    for _ in range(110):
        p = make_random_profile(n_main, rng)
        if p is not None:
            add_candidate(candidates, p)

    for _ in range(10):
        p = make_random_profile(n_main, rng)
        if p is not None:
            add_candidate(candidates, p)
            add_candidate(candidates, p[::-1])
            add_candidate(candidates, sharpen_profile(p, 1.5))
            add_candidate(candidates, sharpen_profile(p, 2.5))

    for d in (4, 8, 16, 32, 64):
        for _ in range(8):
            p = make_block_profile(n_main, rng, d)
            if p is not None:
                add_candidate(candidates, p)
                add_candidate(candidates, p[::-1])

    # Structured finite-field / chirp candidates.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 240.0:
        generate_qr_candidates(candidates, n_main, rng)

    # Transform the currently best raw candidates.
    candidates = [c for c in candidates if np.isfinite(c[0])]
    candidates.sort(key=score_of)

    transformed = list(candidates)
    for item in candidates[:12]:
        sc, p, q = item
        add_candidate(transformed, p[::-1])
        for power in (0.5, 0.75, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
            sp = sharpen_profile(p, power)
            if sp is not None:
                add_candidate(transformed, sp)

    transformed = [c for c in transformed if np.isfinite(c[0])]
    if len(transformed) > 220:
        transformed.sort(key=score_of)
        transformed = transformed[:220]

    if not transformed:
        transformed = [(2.0, np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main), None)]

    transformed.sort(key=score_of)
    top = {}
    out_top = []
    for item in transformed:
        sc = item[0]
        key = round(float(sc), 10)
        if key not in top:
            top[key] = True
            out_top.append(item)
        if len(out_top) >= 12:
            break

    gbest = [np.inf, None, None]
    for item in out_top:
        if gbest[1] is None:
            gbest[0] = float(item[0])
            gbest[1] = item[1].copy()
            gbest[2] = item[2].copy() if item[2] is not None else conv_square(item[1])

    # Local-search the most promising candidates.
    num_runs = min(7, len(out_top))
    for idx in range(num_runs):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
            break
        item = out_top[idx]
        sc, p, q = item
        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left <= 0.0:
            break
        budget = min(420.0, max(80.0, time_left / float(max(1, num_runs - idx))))
        dl = time.monotonic() + budget
        local_search(p, q, sc, dl, rng, gbest)

    # Polish the current global best if time remains.
    refine_count = 0
    prev_best = gbest[0]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0 and gbest[1] is not None and refine_count < 4:
        p = gbest[1].copy()
        q = conv_square(p)
        sc = fast_score(p, q)
        if sc > gbest[0] + 1e-12:
            sc = gbest[0]
        dl = time.monotonic() + min(360.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        local_search(p, q, sc, dl, rng, gbest)
        if abs(gbest[0] - prev_best) < 1e-10:
            # Perturb to avoid repeating an exactly stuck state.
            if gbest[1] is not None:
                pert = rng.normal(size=gbest[1].shape)
                cand = normalize_sequence(gbest[1] * np.exp(0.05 * pert))
                if cand is not None:
                    cq = conv_square(cand)
                    csc = fast_score(cand, cq)
                    if np.isfinite(csc):
                        dl = time.monotonic() + min(240.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
                        local_search(cand, cq, csc, dl, rng, gbest)
        else:
            prev_best = gbest[0]
        refine_count += 1

    # Final authoritative validation among the best few sequences.
    options = []
    if gbest[1] is not None:
        options.append(sequence_from_array(gbest[1]))

    if parent_seq is not None:
        options.append(parent_seq)

    uniform = sequence_from_array(np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main))
    options.append(uniform)

    best_seq = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for seq in options:
        if seq is None or len(seq) == 0:
            continue
        try:
            sc = authoritative_score(seq)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_seq = seq

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = uniform

    final = []
    for x in best_seq:
        xx = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(xx):
            xx = 0.0
        if xx < 0.0:
            xx = 0.0
        if xx > 1000.0:
            xx = 1000.0
        final.append(xx)

    if not final or sum(final) < 0.01:
        final = [0.01] * max(1, n_main)

    # If the submitted best is somehow invalid, evaluate_sequence will reject
    # it; use the safest nontrivial fallback in that case.
    try:
        fs = evaluate_sequence(final)
        if not np.isfinite(fs):
            fallback = [0.01] * max(1, n_main)
            return fallback
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final
```
